[100] (2026-07-07) [#archipelago]: reply to @410cc3aa: Great question.  There's local AI models for all hardware that are reachable over lora with !ai prompts. 
It's already designed to plug into corporate AI with keys.
We're testing @routstr and local/community GPUs with an H200.
We'll also be testing the capabilities of the AMD AI Max cpu. 
#archipelago will do everything it can locally and then reach out to peers for pay as you go services or plug into Claude if subscriptions are your thing.
A separate team is working on automated and AI driven workflows for small farms and homesteads built on archipelago.
The goal is to do far more with less.  Reuse old servers and laptops, leverage off grid frontier trash and edge datacenter models like Sovereign Hybrid Compute.  And ultimately, stay free. 

[101] (2026-07-07) [#archipelago, #freesamourai]: reply to @70c48e4b: Hey friends.  For the past year, everyone has worked for free and completely self-funded #archipelago development.
You will be getting an Alpha shortly but it's time to get real:
To release a successful beta, we need to coordinate more and increase the numbers involved on this project.  Most of this will come in for form of incentivized bounties, creating a hardware compatibility database, paying existing developers, and adding some more resources to the project as well as purchasing hardware to test like the new AMD Halo and more Framework desktops. (Or you simply buy one and help us test).
I'll be talking about this more once the Alpha is out, but please consider helping out.  Archipelago will always be free but how you implement and integrate into your local communities will be something you can get paid for.  Create the next IT Service companies around sovereign tech.  Find local funding for resiliency initiatives.  Get a stall at your local farmers market and talk about big tech overreach and owning your own data while selling graphene phones and openwrt routers.  Survey your area for lora repeaters and join up with local ham radio groups.
We are, right now, taking the first steps into the world we want our grandchildren to grow up in:
A decentralized world with self sovereignty, self determination, and dignity.  No gatekeepers, no middlemen, no more waiting for permission.
Please support Archipelago Beta Release Campaign on nprofile1qy88wumn8ghj7mn0wvhxcmmv9uq32amnwvaz7tmjv4kxz7fwv3sk6atn9e5k7tcqypcvfrjtf2nhsnyjp8nscf8c36utaeyg4rw980atlnlefpcqec7vka5aw3p

https://www.archipelago-foundation.org/
Please support @Ungovernable 
And please please please #freesamourai

[177] (2026-05-12): reply to @Shadrach: Shout out to all the other lotus notes admins on nostr.  ⭐
nevent1qqsqqqr2cjrk0tpnaracm705d5ayuhazff5ya3xsgm55kpswz7sa8nspzemhxue69uhhyetvv9ujuurjd9kkzmpwdejhgq3q5ymzglvv4alrp06q85eqqmawegxf688v0gtqwhjpgtp0a4k2mesqxahtgh

[178] (2026-05-12): reply to @Shadrach: Everything in Lotus Notes is a note, and a database is a collection of notes that are identified by unique IDs. When a note is updated, that note is replicated to other copies of the database. Over time, the obvious performance and reliability problems with this architecture became apparent and the replication process became a lot more sophisticated, but it always worked on this simple logical model of updating the local database and then replicating it to others.

And that was about it—the database full of notes was all there ever was to Lotus Notes. That and an incredible amount of functionality exposed to the user by the fact that notes could incorporate simple scripts, arbitrary programs, workflow rules, and entire GUI layouts. Lotus Notes did not differentiate between "user data" and "program data" at a low level, and indeed users could edit views, write scripts, and build entire applications on top of Lotus Notes that replicated to other users just like an email.

[179] (2026-05-12): Is nostr just a lotus notes reboot?
"It's hard to put a finger on what exactly Lotus Notes was from a modern perspective because the software itself was highly generalized. Wikipedia puts it like this: "Notes and Domino is a cross-platform, distributed document-oriented NoSQL database and messaging framework and rapid application development environment that includes pre-built applications like email, calendar, etc." Let's set aside for the moment the matter of Domino, which we have not introduced, and focus on the parts like "distributed document-oriented NoSQL database" and "messaging framework" which are strange ways to describe a groupware package but actually a very logical way to describe a rapid application development product—mentioned here as just one of the things that Lotus Notes is."
https://computer.rip/2026-03-14-lotusnotes.html

[220] (2026-05-03): “We are torn wide open”

Evolution can be ugly and beautiful, painful and euphoric. An Undying Love For A Burning World is the first new release from Neurosis in a decade, and a potent statement of intent and rebirth - one that marks the first new steps of resolve and resilience.

“We need this, perhaps more than ever, and we suspect we are not alone. The trials and tribulations in our personal lives and as a band, combined with simply trying to navigate the insanity of our society, with the stress, anxiety, and isolation that come with it can be excruciating. Add to that the existential confusion and sorrow of the climate crisis and the sixth mass extinction. It is enough to cause you to completely lose your mind if you can’t find release or catharsis. This strange emotionally charged music has always been our method of trying to survive this and this is what we've always been singing about. When you have spent a lifetime engaged with these energies and utilizing this form of expression to purge and purify, it feels detrimental to our well being to let it sit idle and neglected. This was now or never.”

“We’ve forgotten how to live, so we suffer”

[280] (2026-04-18): reply to @RedTailHawk: The prince of cups is in the past position.  My interpretation was your romantic relationship being the fusion of creative passion with emotional maturity and the resulting balance.

The ten of cups in the present yelled "baby" to me 😃 In a discussion group with @RedTailHawk we were talking about Aries symbol and the female reproductive system, so ten of those was quite loud.  But it also reminds us that you will need to integrate this new being into your life but continue pursuing your own soul path.

The nine of wands is a great card for your future.  The joining of the sun, masculine and the moon, feminine.
"You may feel exhausted, but you are ready to do what you need to accomplish your goals (it can be a long time learning).
You have challenges in front of you, although at the time they do not seem to be complete, those obstacles are just another opportunity to grow. Now, it is the time to trust yourself and continue until you complete what you set out.
As we go deep inside ourselves, we will discover new depths of power that we never thought we had, and these will bring more balance and harmony in our life.
You can be flexible to overcome life challenges. The wind does not break a tree that can bend.
Make sure you set the right boundaries in your life so others cannot exhaust your reserved energy without returning anything and others are clear about how they can assist you at this time.
You may be suspicious at this time (the illusion of the Moon) but you have what it takes to achieve your goals."

[298] (2026-04-07): All addictions follow a similar arc.
So does the cure.
Nostr Anonymous
1 Our common welfare should come first; personal recovery depends on NA unity.
2 For our Group purpose there is but one ultimate authority - a loving God as He may express Himself in our Group conscience, our leaders are but trusted servants, they do not govern.
3 The only requirement for membership is a desire to stop using.
4 Each Group should be autonomous, except in matters affecting other Groups, or NA, as a whole.
5 Each Group has but one primary purpose--to carry the message to the addict who still suffers.
6 An NA Group ought never endorse, finance or lend the NA name to any related facility or outside enterprise, lest problems of money, property or prestige divert us from our primary purpose.
7 Every NA Group ought to be fully self-supporting, declining outside contributions.
8 Nostr Anonymous should remain forever nonprofessional, but our Service Centers may employ special workers.
9 NA, as such, ought never be organized; but we may create service boards or committees directly responsible to those they serve.
10 NA has no opinion on outside issues; hence, the NA name ought never be drawn into public controversy.
11 Our public relations policy is based on attraction rather than promotion; we need always maintain personal anonymity at the level of press, radio and films.
12 Anonymity is the spiritual foundation of all our Traditions, ever reminding us to place principles before personalities

[314] (2026-04-05): reply to @67c23416: Ahh, don’t worry.  This is not about monkeys in the timechain.
Bitcoin transactions are monetary.  Data and storage cost money.  It’s a layer up but they play really nicely.  If you ran a decentralized data center, you might be collecting lightning for storage, cashu for traffic and you settle on the base chain once every 144 blocks.  When that happens you might split some off to the guy who helped you buy the hardware and your tech team.

[315] (2026-04-05): reply to @67c23416: Bitcoin’s incentive model to exist has already been established as the financial record of truth.  With that understanding, we can turn subscription models into usage models.  Pay more for faster AI.  Pay less for a movie you don’t mind watching in an hour versus right now.  We become the infrastructure.   There’s a viable reason to run hardware and software and build out “the network” that runs on top of Bitcoin.
Mempool becomes Netpool.
Tollgate, blossom servers, routing fees, relays.

[419] (2026-02-22): "You are here to bring light to a dark world. It is as simple as that. The
purpose for which wanderers incarnated is all one: to love, and to love,
and to love, and to love. You will be hurt, broken, humiliated and
defeated in the course of a life in faith. It runs directly counter to the
culture in which you live to do things for an ideal reason, to focus upon
the Creator which is unseen rather than all of the phenomena, all of the
gadgetry, all of the amusements that are so delightful upon the surface
of life in your density. Be aware that even in those situations you may
choose to be of service by moving constantly in an awareness of the love
and the light of the one infinite Creator. But also, and most of all, be
aware, we ask each, that non-dramatic service is as vital as dramatic
service, just as the mouth of an entity speaks many things, but would
not be able to function without each and every other organ of the
physical [body], which must be kept in some sort of balance in order
that one may manifest any gifts whatsoever."
-Q'uo

[557] (2025-12-30): reply to @14615a75: I love this scene except for the duvet analogy and misrepresentation as a “comforter”.  I think they could have been more clever.  It’s stood out since I first watched the film.  I wonder if the book is the same.  I knew what a duvet was at the time because I’d used them abroad and found them superior to flat sheet/blankets.  I also knew fancy Americans who bought awful velvet duvet covers and put comforters in them.  
In my head, a good duvet/cover is the most humble, homesteady things you can own and look after. Freshly outdoor aired duvets bring you closer to heaven 🙏

[879] (2025-03-07): If you want to know who taught your favourite sax player..Joe Allard
Allard's analytical approach was combined with a principle he called "inner-hearing" or "pre-hearing"; allowing the sound that is in one's mind to be the guide for tonal control and musical ideas. He first discovered this concept in an illustration from Herbert Spencer's First Principles. As Allard recalls,

The story is told of an artist who created a work based on a picture that was in his mind, what Spencer called the "inner world." And as it came out on canvas, the picture represented the outer world. If the outer world didn't coincide with the inner world, [the artist would] tear it up, throw it in the wastepaper basket and start all over again.

https://www.joeallard.org/pedagogy.html

[960] (2025-02-16): reply to @SoapMiner: Early to the internet, early to irc, early to group chats.  This is hive mind.  This is collective consciousness.
Early to twitter.  The joy of mobile hive nodes sharing and the awakening.
Twitter gave us speech and reach and freedom of will over love or love over will to organize and follow those to brought us joy (or anger).
On twitter during the trucker protest, I would see "1492 new posts" about the trucker protest in the search bar, but when I clicked it.
3
Three Posts.  The same three posts.  PTR PTR PTR.  I knows there's more, it's RIGHT THERE IN FRONT OF ME!  1823 NEW POSTS "trucker protest"
Why can't I see why my fellow global internet twitter users are saying?
That loss....of connection....suddenly 1000's of voices going silent....

Jan6.  Trump de-platformed.  Trucker protest.....
Freedom of reach....dead.  Someone is stopping the flow of information.

Enter nostr. 
 https://m.primal.net/OzDZ.png

[1014] (2025-01-22): reply to @OpnState: Sure, but combined with another piece of legislation on income taxes, it could make it considerable more expensive for me to move abroad.
Currently I guess I'd pay 10-15% more living in Europe, however if the US shifted the tax burden to tariffs, costs would rise in the US but my take home would be more.  Moving over to Europe on the same US wage but paying local taxes would likely make the 10-15% increase more like 40%.
Game theory wise, why would I do that?  It was an appealing idea at 10-15%.

[1025] (2025-01-22): "What I mean is that, like Bitcoin and Tor coming together to spark the revolution that is Silk Road, end-to-end encryption and Tor can come together to spark a communication revolution. There are already products that offer this capability, but they are obscure and unused. What I am envisioning is that technology becoming the standard, but it will require more people understanding why they need it and demanding it from their communications providers. [1026] (2025-01-22): reply to @Deleted Account: I really appreciate cross posting the good info from Twitter. [1053] (2025-01-16): reply to @Blockstream: Unboxed the new  @Blockstream  Jade++ at the phoenixville meetup for show and tell:
+Build quality is great.  Screen is crisp and clear.  Camera works.
-Buttons are not intuitive.  With the original jade, you wheel left and right with your left index finger and press the button with the right thumb.  The Jade++ uses a button for each index finger and you can kind of hold it in this triangle pattern with your left thumb so you can leave your right thumb on the button, but you really just want to hold onto it firmly because it's so sleek and lovely and then you find you are moving your left thumb back and forth from the bottom of the device to the button.

Conclusion.  It's exactly what I was looking for in a simple multivendor multisig setup and far exceeds my expectations....if it was $129
https://m.primal.net/Njab.jpg

[1195] (2024-11-06): There is a beast inside. It awaits a human soul to tame it.

Start with meditation and prayer, because that beast inside needs to experience something beyond itself.

But in prayer and meditation you have not yet met that beast on its own ground.

So next, eat your breakfast like a human being is meant to eat—a step higher than the food you consume, raising it up rather than letting it pull you down.

Then, yes, you have met your human beast on its own ground. But not on its own terms. You are still fighting against its desire to be pulled down into its food.

So then go out into the world and provide goods and services of value, and do that with integrity. At this point, the beast says, “You’re on my territory now, and on my terms, Mr. Soul. I’m the one who knows this game.”

Now you can no longer fight against the human beast. You have to work from within it, with all the talents and skills it has. Because if you don’t, you won’t be providing the value for which others are paying. And that is not integrity.

That is why the very first question asked of the soul when it returns from its mission in this world is not “Did you pray?” but “Did you do business with integrity?”

For that is when you truly made your dent in this vicious world.

https://fxtwitter.com/Chabad/status/1854165211265036390

[1204] (2024-11-03): Items represent the content of a note. Every item has a name, a type, and may have some flags set. A note can have more than one item with the same name. Item types include Number, Number List, Text, Text List, Date-Time, Date-Time List, and Rich Text. Flags are used for managing attributes associated with the item, such as read or write security. Items in design notes represent the programmed elements of a database. For example, the layout of an entry form is stored in the rich text Body item within a form design note. This means that the design of the database can replicate to users' desktops just like the data itself, making it extremely easy to deploy updated applications.

[1254] (2024-09-13): reply to @Blockstream: GM! Genuine non twitter thank you to  @Blockstream
At our meetup yesterday, we ran through their Blockstream Locals program (I'm gonna call it Bitcoin Locals...I know it does nothing for the branding).  Blockstream prepared a decent presentation.  You can check it out here:
https://blockstream.com/local/liquid-education-pack/

We had lots of good questions and most of them were answered in the presentation.

Blockstream generously donated sats to my liquid address and then I split it up 8 ways and sent it to each meetup member even though you only see 2 transactions on the liquid chain:
https://liquid.network/address/VJLKHk2659GvKe7wLpUFnKBve3HHiCdQGesYzLKi3gxJkLeeUSP2mVJ7wycY2mmERGXJWBCLVcGxx7Ev

Interesting note:  I had to wait for each block to clear before I could send the next transaction.  I'm guessing this is because I need wait for my UTXO before I can send the next one.  Because of this it took about 10 minutes to get it all sent.  This would have been faster over lightning obvs.

But even more exciting, we participated in the NonKYC Cigarette economy  npub1563z6kxmvuy7s8zhzan8m0hzmkavyfzg2aw6h7f0fvcvdms398csaxc9n6
 @Phundamentals donated this banging coaster from our friends at  @Ungovernable
And we hopefully all went home a little more free than when we showed up.
https://m.primal.net/Kokt.jpg
